फा० प्रा० 3384. -- यत:, निर्वाचन आयोग का समाधान हो गया है 
कि मार्च, 1972 में हुए बिहार विधान सभा के लिए साधारण निर्वाचन के 
लिए 229-विक्रमगंज निर्वाचन क्षेत्र से चुनाव लड़ने वाले उम्मीदवार श्री 
चन्द्रदीप स्वरूप चौधरी , ग्राम मलौली, शाहाबाद लोक प्रतिनिधित्व 
अधिनियम , 1951 तथा तद्धीन बनाए गए नियमों द्वारा अपेक्षित अपने 
निर्वाचम म्ययो का कोई भी लेखा दाखिल करने में असफल रहे है । 


___ प्रतः, अब , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 10-क के प्रमुमरण में निर्वाचन 
पायोग एतद्द्वारा उक्त श्री सत्य नारायण मिह को मंमद् के किसी भी 
मवन के या किसी राज्य की विधान -सभा प्रथवा विधान परिषद् के सदस्य 
चुने जाने और होने के लिए इस प्रावेश की नारीख में तीन वर्ष की 
कालावधि के लिए मिरहित घोषित करता है । 


पौर यतः, उक्त उम्मीदवार ने , जसे सम्यक सूचना विये जाने पर 
मी , अपनी इस असफलता के लिए कोई कारण प्रपया स्पष्टीकरण नहीं 
पिया है, और, निर्वाचन प्रायोग का यह भी समाधान हो गया है कि 
उसके पास इस असफलता के लिए कोई पर्याप्त कारण या प्यायोचित्य 
महीं है । 


[ सं० विहार-वि० स०/ 229172( 149 ) ] 

ए० एन० सैन , सचिव 


प्रतः, अब , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 10-2 के अनुसरण में निर्वाचन 
पायोग एतद्द्वारा उक्त श्री चन्द्रवीप स्वरूप चौधरी को संसद के किसी भी 
सदन के या किसी राज्य की विधान-सभा अथवा विधान परिषद् के सदस्य 
जुने जाने मोर होने के लिए इस प्रादेश की सारीख से तीन वर्ष की 
कालावधि के लिए निरहित घोषित करता है ।

$ .0 . 3385.---Whereas the Election Commission is satisfied 
that Shri Satya Narain Singh Village Ghusian Khurd P . O . 
Ghusian Kala , Shahabad who was a contesting condidate for 
election to the Bihar Legislative Assembly from 229- Bikram 
ganj constituency held in March , 1972 has failed to lodge 
an account of his election expenses as required by the Repro 
sentation of the People Act , 1951, and the Rules made there 
under ; 


[ सं० बिहार-वि० सं० 22/ 9/72 ( 1 32) ] 


ELECTION COMMISSION OF INDIA 


ORDER 


And whereas, the said candidate even after the due notice 
has not given any reason or explanation for the failure and 
the Election Commission is satisficd that he has no good 
reason or justification for such failure ; 


New Delbi, the 14th October, 1974 


S . O . 3384.- Whereas the Election Commission is satisfied 
that Shri Chandradip Swaroop Choudhary , Village Majhauli 
P . O Koaath , Shahabad who was a contostiog candidate for 
election to the Bihar Legislative Assembly from 229 - Bikram 
gant constituency held in March 1972 has failed to lodge an 
account of his election expenses as required by the Repre 
sentation of the People Act, 1951 , and the Rules made there 
under : 


Now, therefore, in pursuance of section 10A of the said 
Act, the Election Commission hereby declares the said 
Shri Satya Narain Singh to be disqualified for being chosen 
as, and for being, i member of either House of Parliament 
or of the Legislative Assembly or Legislative Council of o 
State for a period of three years from the date of this 
order . 


And whereas, the said candidate even after the due notice 
has not given any reason or explanation for the failure and 
the Election Commission is satisfied that he has no good 
reagon or justification for such failure ;

heing chosen as, and for being, a member of either House

Tho above two petitions are for declaration that the clec 
the 10th Icapondent to the Council of States (Rajya Sabha ) 
from the State of Tamil Nadu in the biennial clection hela

The 6th respondent ( Petitioner in Election Petition No. 
2 of 1974 ) has filed a counter-statement and it is not neces 
gary to set out the contents of the same. 


ELECITON PETITION NO . 1 OF 1974 : 


The contesting respondeat, viz . the 10th respondent, takos 
the stand that for the Hrst time in February , 1974 , on verifi 
calion , he became aware of the fact that his correct date of 
birth was 14 - 5 - 1943 , Up till then , all along , he was under the 
genuine improssion that the date of birth given in the S . S . 
1 . Certificate was the correct date . On this basis , it is said 
that the various school records, the college records and the 
iecords maintained by the Bar Council at the time of his 
corolment , do not bear the correct date of birth , since all the 
records till the completion of his academic carrier arc based 
upon the date of birth as found in the S . $ . L . Certificate , 


Though the book written by this scspondent contains the 
date of birth as 14 - 10 - 1946 , it is only in the biographical data 
and the respondent did not furnish the said information . Tle 
denies that he had not completed 30 years of age , as requi 
red under Article 84 ( b ) of the Constitution on the date of 
nomination . It is further denied that the acceptance of this 
respondent s nomination is vold . 


The case of the petitioner is that nominations for filing 
BLX vacancies to the Council of States ( Rajya Sabha ) from 
the State of Tamil Nadu for the biennial eloctions were called 
for and the Notification in this behalf was issued on 4 - 3 - 1974 , 
fixing the date for receipt of the nominations before 3 P . M . 
on 11 - 3 - 1974 . Eleven nominations were filed and all of them 
were found to be valid on the date of the scrutiny, pamely 
12 - 3 - 1974 . The last date for the withdrawal was 14 - 3 - 1974 
and on that date three candidates withdrew their nomi 
nations, as a result of which eight candidates remained in 
the field . Election was held on 21- 3- 1974 and the counting 
took place on the same day . The results were published declar 
log respondents 4 , 5 and 7 to 10 as elected with the follow 
ing votes : 
Thiru Khader Sha (4th respondent) 

. . . . . . 3 , 500 
Thiru Khaja Mohideen (5th respondent ) .........3,700 
Thiru C . D . Nutarajan (7th respondent) . . . . . . 3 , 500 
Thiru $ . Renganathan (8th respondent ) ... ......4 , 100 
Thiru G . Lakshmanan (9th respondent ) ......... 3,600 
Thiru D . C . John alias Valampuri John 

( 10th respondent) . . . . . . 3,700 
It may be mentioned at this stage that the requisite quota 
to secure the election of a candidate was fixed at 3, 201, 
1. e . 24400 + 1 

- The Petitioner in Election Petition No . 1 of 


The Electoral Roll , 1971, to the North Madras Parlia 
mentary Constituency came to be amended since he made 
a declaration on the basis that he hud actually completed 
30 years 9 months and 25 days . The corrected Electora ) 
Roll will conclusively prove the eligibility of this respon 
dent to seek election and this Court cannot go into the 
correctness or otherwise of the said amendment. Inasmuch 
as no objection was taken to the acceptance of nomination 
of this respondent, it is not open to the election petitioner 
now to contend that the said acceptanco was improper. 
For these reasons, the election Petition is liable to be 
dismissed . 


A reply statement has been flled by the petitioner reiterat 
ing tho allegations contained in the petition . 


RECRIMINATION PETITION NO . 1 OF 1974 ; 


1974 secured nil vote , while the petitioner in Election Peti 
tion No. 2 of 1974 secured 300 votes and consequently, they 
were not declared elected . 


It is the election of the 10th respondent that is attached , 
contending that he has not completed 30 years of age , as 
required under Article 84 ( b ) , on the date of bis nomination 
on 9 - 3 - 1974, since his date of birth is 14 - 5- 1946 . Therefore , 
the acceptance of nominatiop is void . Consequent to the same 
the result of the election has been materially effected by the 
improper acceptance of the nomination and by the non 
compliance of the provisions of the Constitution and the Re 
presentation of the People Act of 1951 and the Rules framed 
thereunder. 


This is a petition under Section 97 read with Section 
83 of the Representation of the People Act, 1951 , and tho 
same has been filod by way of recrimination by tho 6th 
respondent herein ( Petitioner in Election Petition No. 2 of 
1974 ) who has secured 300 votes. It is his case that the 
petitioner in Election Petition No. 1 of 1974 secured no 
votes from the clectorato and cannot be declared to have 
been elected under section 101 of the Representation of 
the People Act, 1951 , by this Court. The voting and the 
counting of votes for this election were as per chapters 6 
und 7 of the Conduct of Flections Rules and the procedure 
for counting of votes has been detailed in the Schedule to 
the Conduct of Elections Rules, 1961, as per Rules 83 
therein . There being six soats to ho filled up and five other 
candidates having secured votes of the required quota , 
were declared elected . In so far as the petitioner in this 
recrimination netition has secured 300 original votes. He 
alone should be declared elected in the event of the elec 
tion of the lith respondent heing set aside, and the peti 
tioner in Election Petition No 1 of 1974 cannot be declared 
to have been elected at all to the sixth seat, in view of the 
above provision .

Exhibit R . 4 was not even put to R . W . 2 in the chief 
examination , and ultimately It fell to the lot of the Court 
to elicit from him whether that was the original of them 
Baptism Register, and being a true Christian , RW . 2 obliged 
to accept that it was not the original but it was re -written , 
though he was highly interested in helping the respondent. 
His interestedness in the respondent is evident from 
the fact that he came from Tuticorin to Madray 
alongwith R . W . 3 , the elder brother of the respon 
dent and was staying with him , and above all, the telegram 
addressed to RW . 2 , requiring him to come and give ev l 
dence in this case is produced by R . W . 3 , the elder brother 
of the respondent, from his pocket. 


The evidence of the R . W . 5 is unworthy of acceptance 
and the Icast that is said about him the better. He com 
mitted " an unholy Act" by burning the original of the 
Baptism Register while the Canon Law requires him to 
preserve the same. 


( 5 ) The Marriage Register, Exhibit P . 29 , was summoned 
to be produced in Application No . 2095 of 1974 and that 
mentions Rev . Fr. N . A . Joseph or Rev . Fr. Swami. But 
neither has bech examined to prove the contents of these 
documents , Nor did they produce the game, but it was 
produced by another person. Besides , it is the signature of 
RW . 3 alone that was marked and in so far as the other 
entrice have not been marked , they cannot be looked into , 
especially the date of Baptism . If the petitioner Was keen 
on relying on this document, he should have proved the 
same by letting in evidence as to who made the various 
entries , more so , when an objection was raised to its ad 
Missibility and the persons who could bave proved these 
entries were available. In elaborating this submission , it is 
contendod that in so far as Exhibit P . 29 ( a ) , the signature 
alone has been marked , it should be held that the signature 
has been proved . In such a case , the ratio laid down in 
Madholai Singhu v . Asian Assurance Co Ltd ., and others 
( A . I. R . 1954 , Bombuy , 305) and Asa Ram v . Emperor 
( A . I. R . 1933, Allahabad , 593 ) would apply . The probative 
value of the marriage Register concerning the entry relating 
to baptism is limited , since the object of maintaining the 
Register is the registration of marriage and not baptisnı, 
and whatever is not required to be filled will not be relevant 
under Section 35 of the Evidence Act. G . 0 , No . 40 , Eccle 
siastical, dated 30th June, 1924 , as seen from the Appendix 
set out at page 864 of the Gazette , does not contain any 
column relating to baptism and if that was so , the same was 
not required to be filled up, and any form which does not 
conform to thc statute canot he relied upon . Nagavasami 
Naidu and others v . Kochadai Naidu and others ( A . I.R . 
1969 , Madras, 329 ) is an authority for this proposition , 
Likewise , the decision reported in Maniklal Shah v . Hiralal 
Show ( A . I. R . 1950 , Calcutta , 377) . Moreover, as admitted 
by the petitioner himself, this is an altered entry , which is 
scen by a mere look at the entry , and the Petitioner should 
have explained the circumstances under which the alteration 
came to be effected , and in this view also the non - examina 
tion of the persons who were in charge of maintaining these 
records and registers becomes very vital. 

(6 ) Though the Birth Register for the year 1943 has been 
summoned , it can be relied on only if it contains an entry 
relating to the date of birth , since the absence of an entry 
cannot be relied on . It has been repeatedly held so in many 
coses and two of them would be sufficient for this purpose, 
vit., The Queen Empress v . Gress Chunder Bancriee ( I . L . R . 
10, Calcutta , 1024 ) and Ali Nasir Khan v. Manik Chand 
and another ( I. L . R . 25, Allahabad , 90 at 100 ) . Nor can 
It be concluded that merely because there is no entry in 
the 1943 register that the respondent was not born in 1943 , 
since there is every likelihood of the birth not having been 
reported at all and the admission of P . W . 13 , the Sub -Regist 
rar , would show that the Village Munsif fills up the Revister 
only if births and deaths are reported . The non -availability 
of the 1946 Register does not in any manner affcct his cașc . 


In meeting these arguments, Mr. K . K . Venugopal, learned 
counsel for the respondent, makes the following submissions : 


( 1 ) It is possible for the petitioner to have let in direct 
evidence by summoning Parsons from the village or some 
relations when the event, namely the birth of the respon 
dent, had taken place only about 30 years ago , and inasmuch 
as the same has not been let in , the best evidence has not 
been adduced . 


( 2 ) It is the categoric stand of the respondent, as seen 
from paragraph 3 of the counter -statement, that it was only 
for the first time in February , 1974 , on verification hc be 
came aware of the fact that his correct date of birth Wils 
14 - 5 - 1943 . Up till then , all along, he was under the genuine 
impression that the date of birth given in his S .S . L . C . book 
was the correct date. This, taken together with the other 
statements in pasagraphs 16 to 18 of his counter -statement 
in Election Petition 1 of 1974 , would clearly show that the 
mistake in age was carried forward , from one register to 
another, 


( 3 ) The school records and the college records, in order 
that they may be admissible under Section 35 of the Evi 
dence Act , ( 1 ) should have been maintained by a public 
servant or by a person statutorily authorised to maintain such 
records, and ( ii) the circumstances under which such an 
entry came to be made and the source of information for 
making such an entry should be given and in 90 far as 
the same has not been done, they are of no evidentiary value 
at all. The school that maintained Exhibits like P . 80 and 
P . 81, admittedly is a private school and there was no statu 
tory duty to maintain them ; therefore they cannot be relled 
on , as has been held a Gopalan y . Kannan ( 14ELR, 458 ), 
This apart, the circumstances under which the entry relating 
to the date of birth of the respondent came to be entered , 
or the person who gave the information , has not been extil 
mined, since the application form for admission , which 
would have furnished the source of information , is not avail 
able . P . W . 8 , the Headmaster of the S . Marysi Elementary 
School, admits that he did not maintain Exhibit P . 80 . Nor 
does he have any personal knowledge as to how and when 
those entries were made. It is further admitted by him that 
he is not aware of the fact as to who made an application 
for admission into the First Class . In a similar case reported 
in N . Sankara Reddy V . Yashoda Reddy ( 13 ELR . 34 ) it 
was held that in the absence of the source of information , 
the school register should not be relied on and the said 
decision fully supports his submission , 


( 7 ) Exhibit P . 28 cannot be pressed into service against 
the respondent, since the case of the respondent is, he did 
not supply the information relating to the birth to be in 
corporated in the biographical sketch . The evidence of R . W . 
4 is specific in that the respondont found fault with R . W . 
4 for having published the wrong month . This evidence is 
consistent with the case of the respondent, as set out in 
paragraph 3 of his counter- statement that all the while he 
was labouring under the misapprehension that his date of 
birth was only 14 - 5 - 1946 , unaware of the real date of birth , 
which is 14 - 5 - 1943 . It is not uncommon in this country to 
glve wrong dates of birth and that is why in the State and 
Subordinate Services Rules , Rule 49 ( h ) specifically provides 
for the procedure for the alteration . Likewise , Rule 145 ( 3 ) 
of the Railway Establishment Code, Vol. I, also enables a 
railway servoni to have his date of birth altered. As laid 
down in Rrlj Mohan Singh v . Priya Brar Narain Sinha and 
others (i ) A . T. R . 1965 (SC ) 282 , the present case should be 
viewed in that back - ground. If the mistaken date was car 
ried forward , As Spoken to by R . W . 1 from one record to 
another , it cannot be contended that these records should he 
accepted . on the contrary they have little evidentiary value. 


(4 ) Since this mistaken date of birth came to be entered 
in the S .S .L . Certificate and College Records and Bar Council 
records , their probative value is little , though they may he 
public records , 


( 8 ) In the instant case there is clear oral evidence of RW . 
2 that the Respondent was baptised in the year 1943 and he 
was an infant, one week old . He speaks from out of his 
memory about this baptism , since his father was a catechist

THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : DECEMBER 28 , 1974 /PAUSA 7. 1896 


was accepted by the Election Tribunal which was rejected 
by the High Court as untristworthy. Under those circum 
stances, their Lordships of the Supreme Court held : 


trary , which is so in the present case , thelr evidentiary valuo 
is of the highest order. All that the respondent states with 
reference to these documents is that the mistake in the entry 
in the second register regarding the date of birth has been 
carried forward from one record to another , and any 
document which contains an entry relating to his date of birth 
other than 14 - 5 - 1943 is incorrect. A similar thing happened 
in N . Sankara Roddi v. Yashoda Reddi( 1 ) ( 13 E . L .R . 34 ) 
also . In his submission , the case on hand should be viewed 
in the background of the decision reported in Brij Mohan 
Singh v . Priya Brat Narain Singh and Others ( ? ) ( A .I. R . 
1965 (SC ) 282 ) , where also a wrong entry made in the ad 
mission register was carried over to the Matriculation certi 
ficate and the same was adhered to in the later records. 1 
may at once point out that there is absolutely no foundation 
for this submission , since there is no pleuding at all to the 
cffect that the original entry in the school register was a 
wrong cntiy and the lower age was given or the academic 
age was purposefully minimised by the parents or guardian 
in order to gain some advantage for the student, namely the 
respondent. All that he states in paragraph 3 of his counter 
statement in Election Petition No. 1 of 1974 is : 


" However much one may condemn such an act of 

making a false statement of age with a view to ge 
cure an advantage in getting public service , a judge 
of facts cannot ignore the position that in actual 
life this happens not infrequently . We find it im 
possible to say that the Flection Tribunal was 
wrong in accepting the appellant s cxplanation . Tak 
ing all the circumstances into consideration . Wo 
are of opinion that the explanation might very well 
be truc . . . " . 


“ 3 . This respondent subinity that it was for the first 

time in February 1974, on verification , he become 
aware of the fact that bis correct date of birth 
was 14 - 5 - 1943. Up till then , all along, he was 
under a genuinc impression that the date of birth 
given in his S .S . L . C . Book was the correct date " . 


I may now usefully refer to the decision reported in 
Manohar Naik v . Binode Behari Bariha ( 1 ) (23 E . L . R . 
379 ) , wherein 
" The first respondent s election was challenged on the 

ground, inter alia that his age was less than 25 
ycuis, at the time of clection . The Tribunal, relying 
on tho horoscope evidence as against the date of 
birth recorded in the School Certificate , held that 
the respondent was more than 25 ycars of age on 
the date of his election . In Appcal, it was held that 
there was no evidence on behalf of the respondent 
to negative the accuracy of the school age as re 
presenting the real age . The respondent had merely 
averred that the matriculation certificate could not 
be taken as conclusive proof of the fact in issuo . 
Apart from the other evidence showing the genu 
‘ineness of the entry of the age in the School 
Certificate the publication of the matriculation re 
sult with the age and description of the candidato 
Is a fact of public nature . The court has to form 
an opinion as to the existence of this fact and the 
recital of age contained in the Orissa Gazette is a 
relevant fact. The Guzette itself is presumed to be 

genuine under s . 81 of the Evidence Act" . 
I may also take note of a similar contention advanced in 
this case , which was dealt with at page 286 in paragraph 
15 : 


From this and the other statements made in paragraphs 16 , 
17 and 18 of the counterstatement in Election Petition No. 
1 of 1974 , Mr. Venugopal wants to contend that there are 
enough statements to basc this submission . I am unable 
to agree. Apart from there being not a specific pleading, 
even in the evidence, neither the respondent ( R . W .) 1 
nor his elder brother ( R . W . 3) would say that the date of 
birth given at the time of the respondent s admission into the 
school was cither wrong or incorrect. In N . Sankara Reddi 
v . Yashoda Reddi ( 1 ) ( 13 E .L . R . 34 ) , no doubt the birth 
register was produced which was accepted . In the later part 
of my judgment I will show as to how evidence is lacking to 
hold against this admission , and in my view N . Sankara 
Roddi v . Yashoda Reddi s ( 1) ( 13 E . L .R . 34 ) caso has no 
application . In fact , R . W , 1 states as follows : 


" Q . After you passed the S .S . L . C . whọ furnished the 

age particulars to the Collego ? 


A . There was a certificate . From one certificate to an 

other, it has been carried on throughout. 


Q . Who has furnished the particulars to the Bar Coun 


cil ? 


A . I furnished it . 


Q . Based on what? 


A . I furnished the particulars based on the first page 

of the S .S . L . C . 


" On the basis of A . I. R . 1941, Calcutta , 41, A . I. R . 1952 , 

Punjab , 277 , and 3 Elec . Law Reports, 31 , Mr. 
Patnaik contents that the date of birth ag ontered 
in the school register of Kuchinda and as found in 
Ex. 14 , though admissible, is not of great videntiary 
value as there is no evidence to show as to on 
what matcrials this entry in the admission register 
about the age of respondent No . 1 was made. Even 
on such a proposition , appellant will succeed if 
there is evidence of some weight on his sido and 
no evidence to the contrary on behalf of the ros 
pondent No. 1 . . . . In A . I. R . 1957 . Calcutta , 211 
A . I. R . 1957, Kerala 103, and A .I. R . 1940 , Nagpur , 
217 , it has been held that Matriculation Certificate 
and School admission register are admissible as 
proof of the age of the person concerned . In 
1937 , Kerala , 103, observation had gone to the 
extent that the entry thus made in official record 
by a public servant in discharge of his official duty 
has to be presumed as corect in the absence of 

other reliable evidence to the contrary ” . 
These observations in my view , apply with full force to the 
facts of the present case. 

T . E . L . C . Kabis High School Records : Exhibits P . 11 and 
P . 11 ( a ) and P . 12 and P . 12 ( a ) arc the 1ccord maintain 
ed by the T . E . L . C . Kabis High School and they have been 
spoken to by P . Ws, 11 and 12 , P . W . 11 says that Exhibits 
P . 11 and P . 12 were prepared by the then Headmasters and 
he is familiar with the signatures of those Headmasters . Hero 
again the date of birth is as contended by the petitioner . 


Q . Likewise in various other University Examination 

applications, you have furnished the particulars ? 
A . Yes. I furnished the particulars". 


From the above evidencc it is obvious that there is not even 
# suggestion that the entry was wrong . No explanation has 
been offered as to how and why such an incorrect entry has 
been given . Was it by mistakes , inadvertance or deliberate 
or designed ? One is obliged to guess. “ It is no function of 
a Judge to be lachrymose over what might have beca brought 
in evidence. His function is to go by evidence that cxists" , 
in the words of Bijayesh Mukherji, J. (Vide Babulall Chou 
khani V . Caltex ( India ) Ltd . ( 1 ) ( A . I. R . 1967 (Calcutta ) 
205). In the Supreme Court case relied upon, namely Brij 
Mohan Singh v . Priya Brat Narain Sinha and Others( 2 ) 
( A . I.R . 1963 (S . C .) 282), the case of the appellant, as seen 
from paragraph 20 , at page 287, is that date of birth , viz ., 
October 15 , 1937, was an incorrect statement made at the 
request of the person who went to get admitted to the 
school. Thc lequest was made to make him appear two 
yoars younger than he really was, so that later in life he 
would have an advantage when seeking public service for 
which a minimum age for eligibility is often prescribed . This 


Book - " Varalatıil Kalaignar" : Exhibit P . 28 is the book 
written by the respondent in which is contained a biographical 
sketch and the date of birth mentioned there is 14 - 10 - 1946 . 
R . W . 4 is his evidence states that the particulars icgarding 
this biographical sketch were given by the respondent and it 
was he who wrote down , and at the time the book was pub

Issue No. 2 in Election Petition 1 and 2 of 1974 : 


In paragraph 28 and 29 of the counter -statement of the 
respondent in Election Petition No. I of 1974 and in para 
graph 49 of the counter- statement in Election Petition No. 2 
of 1974 , objections have been raised as to the admissibility 
of Annexure 1 to 7 . A further ground is raised that these 
Annexures are liable to be struck off as they werc not filed 
with the election petitions. But, on a letter verification from 
the records maintained by the Office of this Court, Mr. 
Venugopal submitted that he was satisfied that these Anne 
xures were hled along with the clection petitions , and hence 
this objection does not survive . A regards their admissibility , 
since the petitioner did not scck to rely on any of these docu 
ments that question does not arişç . Thus issue No. 2 19 ans 
wered in favour of the petitioner. 


( 2 ) There are valuablc admissions made by the respon 
dont under signed declarations, a list of which I have yet 
out above , which cannot be got over by a simple explanation 
that thc wrong date hue been carried forward from one re 
cord to another, for which , as shown carlier, there is no 
basis at all, much less any evidence . The petitioner has 
taken all possible stops (vide Exhibits P - 48, P - 49 , P - 52 , to 
P - 37 and P -60 to P -63 ) to produce the best evidence possi 
blo, as a result of which along all these documentary evi 
dence have come to Court. In fact , I should say thut no 
effoit has been wanting from the petitioner in this regard , 
And therefore , it is futile to contend that the burden of proof 
has not been discharged . I may , in this connection , use 
fully refer to the observations of the Supreme Court in 
Narayan Bhagwantrao Gosavi Balajiwala v . Gopal Vinayak 
Gosavi and others ( 1 ) A . I. R . 1960 ( SC ) 100 ( 1960 SCR 
773 at 782 ) . 
" The burden of proof is of importance only where , by 

reason of not discharging the burden , which was 
put upon it , a party must eventually fail . Where , 
however the parties have joined issue and have 
led evidence and the conflicting evidence can be 
weighed to determine which way the issue can be 
decided , the abstract question of burden of proof 
becomes academic " , 


( 3 ) The documentary and oral evidence on the side of 
the respondent are liable to be rejected . 


From the above, the irresistible conclusion is that the 
respondent was born in the year 1946, which would mean 
that he had not completed 30 years of age on the date of 
the scrutiny of nominations, as required under Article 84 
( b ) of the Constitution . Not to hold so , would amount to 
shutting one s eyes to reality plain to be seen and accepting a 
mythical baptism in the year 1943. 


[ sque Nos. 7 , 8 , 9, and 11 in Election Petition No. 2 of 
1974 : 

The allegation relating to corrupt practice is not specific . 
What the petitioner states , in effect is , by filing an amended 
Electoral Roll, the respondent baş managed to have his nomi 
nation accepted . The Returning Officer, as is well known , can 
only look into the Electoral Roll and he cannot go behind 
it. When the respondent produced Exhibit R - 2 , the certified 
extract, the Returning Officer hos no option than to accept 
the nomination on the strength of the Electoral Roll . Under 
Section 36 ( 2 ) of the Representation of the people Act, 1951, 
the Returning Officer has got to examine the nomination paper 
and decide all objections that may be made to any nomination . 
In so far as no objection had been raised be would have had 
no opportunity to go into this question . Nor can he suo moto 
embark upon an enquiry , since he would have had no reason 
to conclude that the respondent was under aged with the 
corrected Electoral Roll before him . In fact, in similar circum 
stances, the Supreme Court has held in Durga Shankar Mehta 
v . Thakur Reghuraj Singh and others ( I) A .I.R . 1954 (SC ) 
520 , at page $ 24 : " when the candidato appcars to be pro 
perly qualified on the face of the electoral roll and the nomi 
nation paper and no objection is raised to the nomination , 
the Returning Officer has no other alternative but to accept 
the nomination . This would be apparent from Section 36 
( 7 ) of the Act. . . .. . . .. . In other words, the electoral roll is 
conclusive us to the qualification of the electorate except 
where a disqualification is expressly alleged or proved , " In 
view of this , it cannot be held that the respondent is guilty 
of any corrupt practice . Even if thc respondent has given a 
false declaration, that is a matter which arises under the Re 
presentation of the People Act of 1950 and Section 31 speci. 
fically deals with the same. Sitting as an Election Tribunal 
all that I can enquire into is whether thero has been any non 
observance of the provisions of this Act or of any rule or 
ordors made under This Act. as laid down in Section 100( 1 ) 
( d ) ( iv ) of the Representation of the People Act, 1951. Hence 
these issues are answered against the petitioner. 


The framers of the Constitution fixed the age for elec 
tion to the Rajya Sabha at 30 so that men of mature wis 
dom and rich experience might be the Members and that is 
why they are called " the elders " . This court must be jealous 
of persons trying to circumvent this salutary principle and 
Any usurpation of this high office must be prevented and 
the purity of elections must be maintained , since on that 
alone real democracy can flourish . 

Thus, I answer issue No. 3 in both the petitions in fa 
vour of the petitioner . 


Rogarding issue No. 7 in E . P . No. 1 of 1974 (issue No . 
10 in E . P . No. 2 of 1974 ) I must state that Mr. Venugopal 
does not seriously contend thut merely becausc tho objec 
tion was not raised about the disqualification at the time of 
the scrutiny of his nomination , the petitioner would be pre 
cluded from doing so in the election petition . As pointed 
out by the Supreme Court in Durga Shankar Mehta v . 
Raghuraj Singh and Others ( 1 ) , D . I. R . 1954 (SC ) 520 this 
is a fundamental disability in the candidate to stand for the 
election at all. Hence this issue is also answered in favour 
of the petitioner . 


Issue No. 4 Election Petitions 1 and 2 of 1974 : 

In view of my finding on issue No . 3 in the respective elec 
rion petitions, I hold that the election of the 10th respondent 
is liable to be declared as void under Section 100 ( 1 ) ( a ) and 
( 1 ) ( d ) (iv ) of the Representation of the People Act, 1991. 


Issue No . 5 in Election Petition 1 and 2 of 1974 ,- - It has 
been repeatedly laid down by the Supreme Court that this 
Court sitting as a Tribunal, hag no jurisdiction to enquire Into 
the correctness or otherwise of the amendment made to the 
Electoral Rolls . But I may just in passing observe that the 
amendment to the Electoral Roll has come to be carricd 
out in a haphazard fashion by P . W . 18 . In his application , 
whlle reproducing the then existing Flectoral Roll , the 
respondent had conveniently omitted his age, but would give 
only as to how his age should be corrected to read as 31 . 
The records, Exhibits P - 73 to P - 76 do not show on what 
material the amendment came to be effected . Thus, I ans 
wer the issue accordingly .

Apart from the preamble referring to the Houses of parlia 
ment, thicre arc also other Sections from which it can be casily 
gathered that the election to the Rajya Sabha cannot be ex

Mr. Chitlae , learned counsel appcaring for the petitioner in 
Election Petition No . 2 of 1974 , contends that in the event of 
the election of the respondent being set aside, the Couit has no 
other choice except to declare the petitioner in Election Peti 
lion No. 2 to have been validly elected to the vacancy caused . 
His submission is that the petitioner in Election Petition No. 1 
obtained nil votes . Hence , he stands automatically eliminated . 
If that be so , the only remaining candidate in the field , having 
sccuired 300 votes , is the petitioner in Election Petition No. 2 
of 1974 . Therefore , his election is " Hobson s Choice " . This 
is the position factually , and the decision in Viswanatha Reddy 
v . Konappa Rudrappa Nadqouda and anotber ( 1 ) A . I. R . 
1969 (SC ) 604 , supports his submission . Even in law the 
same position is arrived at since Rule 81 ( 2 ) of the Conduct 
of Elcctions Rules, 1961 (hereafter icferred to as the Rules) 
states : 


( b ) examine all the ballot papers in his parcel and sub 

parcels, arrange the unexhausted papers in sub 
parcels according to the next available preferences 
recorded thereon for the continuing candidates , 
count the number of papers in each such sub 
parcel and credit it to the candidate for whom 
such preference is recorded , transfer the sub - par 
cel to that candidate , and make a separate sub 

parcel of all the exhausted papers ; and; 
(c ) see whether ny of the continuing candidates lias , 

after such transfer and credit, secured the quota ." 
Rule 78 speaks of candidates who obtain the necessary quo 
til . be declared electei. 

Rule 79 speaks of transfer of surplus . 


" 81 ( 2 ) . When at the end of any count only one vacancy 

remains unfilled and the value of the papers of some 
one candidate exceeds the total value of the papers 
of all the other continuing candidates together with 
any surplus not transferred , that candidate shall be 
declared clected ." 


Petitioner in Election Petition No. 2 of 1974 being such a 
candidute contemplated under the said Rulc , he should te 
declared deleted . Per contra Mr. Venugopal argues that it is 
not correct to state that merely because the election petitioner 
in Election Petition No . 1 had obtained nil votes in the first 
count, he does not stand automatically eliminated. He is still 
a continuing candidato within the meaning of Rule 71 ( 1 ) . 
Moreover , inasmuch as six seats came to be filled at the first 
count itself, sincc cach of the candidates who was declared 
elected , obtained the required quote of 3 , 201 votes , the stage 
for applying Rule 81( 2 ) did not arise at all and in such a case 
it will be in the realm of a guess or speculation that the peti 
tioner in Election petition No. 2 got required quota and the 
observations of the Supreme Court in R . M . Seshadri v . 
G . Vasantha Pai ( 1 ) A . I. R . 1969 ( SC ) 692 at 701 arc of great 
help to him . The arguments of Mr. Chitlae, though apparent 
ly attractive, appear to me, on closer examination to be un 
tenable . Part VII of the Rules lays down the procedure for 
counting of votes at clections by Assembly Members or in 
Council, which is the one that is applicable to the present case , 
I may reproduce the material portions of Rule 71, which have 
a bearing on the present issue. 


Rule 80 states the methud of cxclusion of candidates lowest 
on the poll, und Rule 81 ( 1 ) and ( 2 ) speak of filing the last 
vacancies. 

If only the petitioner in Election Petition No . I ceases to be 
a continuing candidate , the petitioner in Election Petition No. 2 
could be declared to have been elected . It is not correct to 
state that merely because he obtained nil votes in the first 
count, he ceases to he a continuing candidate. Ouly in the 
frst count he has obtained nil votes . If the second count had 
been held , for which there was no necessity in this case , it 
might have so happened that he secured more votes than tho 
pentitoner in Election Petition No. 2 . The process of exclu 
sion contemplated under Rule 80 would arise after the trans 
fer of surplus, which transfer of surplus is detailed out under 
Rule 79. In the present case that stage has not arisen at Jl. 
That being the position , there is no possibility of applying 
Rulc 81. The votes cast in favour of the respondent are not 
thrown away votes , but aro valid votes, in which case the 
petitioner in Election Petition No. 2 of 1974 cannot claim 
that he had obtined the majority of votes. Even assuming 
that they are thrown away votes, the petitioner in Election 
Petition No . 2 of 1974 had not established that either he has 
obtained the required quota or is the sole continuing candi 
daite . The question of obtaining majority of votes cannot 
arise at all in the case of a single transferable vote and it is 
only by obtaining the quota , which quota is ascertained under 
Rule 76 , the candidate gets elccted . 


“ 71 DEFINITIONS : In this part, 
( 1 ) continuing candidate means any candidate not 

elected and not excluded from the poll at any given 
time; 


( 2) count means 
(a ) all the operations involved in the counting of the 

first preferences recorded for candidates ; or 


In Viswanatha Reddy v . Konappa Rudrappa Nadquoda and 
another ( 1) A .I. R . 1969 ( SC ) 604 what was held was where 
there are only two contesting candidates and one of thten is 
linder a statutory disqulifiction , votes cast in favour of the 
disqualified candidate may be regarded as thrown away , ir 
respective whether the voters who had voted for him were 
aware of the disqualification and no fresh poll is necessary . It 
may be necessary to note that the above decision of the Sup 
reme Court related to a case of single non -transferable vote. 
But that is not the casc here . As I said above, it is only by 
obtaining the required quota the candidato gets clected , 


( b ) all the operations involved in the transfer of the 

surplus of an elected candidate ; or 


( c ) all the operations involved , in the transfer of the 

tolal value of votes of an excluded candidate ; 


I may now set out the observations of the Supreme Court 
rendered in R . M . Seshadri y , G . Vasantha Pai ( 1 ) A . I. R . 
1969 (SC ) 692 at page 701, which are apposite : 


( 5 ) original votc , in relation to any candidate , many it 

vote derived from a ballot paper 01) which it first 

preferred is recorded , for such candidate ; 
(6 ) surpl,19 means the number by which the value cf the 

votes , original and transferred , of any candilato er 
c¢¢ds the quota ; 


( 7 ) transferred vote , in relation to any candidate , means 

a vote the value or the part of the value of which 
is credited to such candidate and which is derived 
from the ballot paper on which a second or a guh 
sequent preference is recorded for such candidate ; 


" It remains o consider the argument of Mr. Gupta whe 

ther Vasanth Pai could be declared elected . This 
will depend on our reaching the conclusion that but 
for the fact that voters were brought through this 
corrupt practice to the polling booths, the result of 
the election had been ineterially affected . In a single 
transferable vote , it is very difficult to say how the 
voting could have gone, because if all the votes wbich 
Seshadri had got, had gone to one of the other can 
didate who got eliminated at the earlier counts, those 
candidates would have won . We cannot orders a

* * राष्ट्रीय कृषि ऋण ( पीर्घकालीन प्रवर्तन ) निधि और राष्ट्रीय प्रौद्योगिक ऋण ( वीर्घकालीन प्रवर्तन ) निधि मे मे किये गये निषेण शामिल नही है । 

राष्ट्रीय कृषि ऋण ( दीपकालीन प्रवर्तन ) निधि में प्रदत्त ऋण और अग्रिम शामिल नहीं । परन्तु राज्य सरकारो को दिये गये अस्थायी मोबरड्राफ्ट 
शामिल है । 
+रिजर्व बैंक प्राफ इडिया अधिनियम की धारा 17( 4 ) ( ग ) के अधीन अनुचित पाणिज्य बैंकों को मीयादी बिलो पर अग्रिम दिये गये 17, 05, 00 ,000 
रुपये शामिल है । 
राष्ट्रीय कृषि ऋण ( दीर्घकालीन प्रवर्तन ) निधि और राष्ट्रीय कृषि ऋण (स्थिरीकरण ) निधि से प्रवरस ऋण और प्रनिम शामिल नहीं हैं ।

का० प्रा० 3394 --- केन्द्रीय उत्पाद शुल्क नियमावली 1944 के नियम 5 
द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुये म पार० एम० शुक्सा, समाहर्ता, 
केन्द्रीय उत्पाद शुल्क नागपुर एतद्द्वारा नागपुर केन्द्रीय उत्पाद शुल्फ समा . 
हर्ताक्षेत्र के निम्नलिखित मारणी के स्तम्भ 4 में वर्शाये गये अधिकारियों 
को अपने अपने अधिकार क्षेत्र में म्लभ 3 में उल्लिखित विद्युत अथवा 
भाप की सहायता के बिना प्रक्रियागत सूती वस्त्र के मम्बन्ध में विशेष 
कार्यविधि से सम्बन्धिन केन्द्रीय उत्पाद शुल्क नियमावली 1944 के नियमों 
के अन्तर्गन ममाहर्ता की शक्तियो का प्रयोग करने का अधिकार देता है ।

का० प्रा० 339 3.- - इस समाहर्तालय की दिनाक 20-7-72 की 
माधिसूचना सं० 1/ 72 की अनुसूची में निम्नलिखित मशोधन किये जायेगे । 

2. मद सं० 5, विशाखापत्तनम आई० डी० मो० के मामने निम्न 
लिखित रूप में संशोधन किये जाये । 
( i) स्तम्भ म . 4 मे , श्री फाकुलम जिले पार्षथीपुरम , योब्बिली 

और सम्पूर्ण मालुर तालुको के स्थान पर निम्नलिखित शब्द 
प्रतिस्थापित किये जायें । 
" पाधिपेन्टा फिरके को छोड़कर पूरा माल र तालुका, बोखिमली 

सानुक प्रौर कुरूपम फिरके को छोड़कर सम्पूर्ण पार्वषी 

पुरम एष बोब्बिली तालुका " 
( ii ) " यलमचिली तालु के येलमनचिली और रिमिली और लालम 

कोपर " शादी के स्थान पर निम्नलिखित शब्द प्रतिस्थापित 
किये जायें । 
" येलमचिली तालुके के पेडापल्लि और कोक्फिरापल्लि गावों को 

छोड़कर लालमको डर , डिमिली और येलमंचिली फिरके " 
( iii ) इस कार्यालय की दिनाक 20- 7- 72 की अधिसूचना स० 1/ 72 

की दिनांक 15- 4- 74 के अनुमन्ध में जोड़े गये शब्दो 
अर्थात् पादेश तालुफ के कानीगाडा और पादेरु फिरके के तथा 
गजपथिंगरम तालुके के स्थान पर निम्नलिखित शब्द प्रतिस्थापित 
किये जायेंगे ।

का०मा० 3395, हज समिति अधिनियम 1959 के खंड 12 ( 1 ) 
द्वारा भारत सरकार को प्रवत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुये , 1 अप्रैल 1975 
में हज समिति , बम्बई में कार्यकारी अधिकारी का पद एतद्द्वाग 
र० 1200- 50-1600 के वेतनमान में बढ़ा दिया गया है, जिसका 
दर्जा भारत मरकार में अवर सचिव के दर्जे के बराबर होगा । 


का० प्रा० 3396. -निर्यात ( क्वालिटी नियन्त्रण और निरीक्षण ) 
अधिनियम , 1963( 1963 का 22 ) की धारा 17 द्वारा प्रदन पक्तियो 
का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार मिलाई मशीन निर्यान ( क्वालिटी 
नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण ) नियम , 1967 में और आगे मशोधन के लिये 
निम्नलिखित नियम एतद्वारा बनाती है, 
____ 1 ( 1 ) इन नियमो का नाम मिलाई मशीन प्रायात ( क्वालिटी 
नियन्त्रण और निरीक्षण ) मशोधन नियम , 1974 है । 


2. श्री एम० एच० सिद्दिकी को एतद्वारा हज समिति, बम्बई में 
कार्यकारी अधिकारी नियुक्त किया गया है । वह विदेश मंत्रालय में अपर 
मचिव के पद से मेवा निवृत्त होने पर , 1 अप्रैल 1975 में ही कार्यकारी 
अधिकारी के पद का कार्यभार सभालेगे । जिस तारीख से श्री सिद्धिकी 
पद का कार्यभार सभालेगे उसी तारीख से इस मंत्रालय से मऊदी अरब 
में रयात पोर मुसाफिर खाना से सम्बद्ध कार्य हज ममिति के कार्यकारी 
अधिकारी को स्थानान्तरित कर दिया जायेगा परन्तु यह पूर्ण रूप से इस 
मंत्रालय के नियंत्रण में रहेगा । 


( 2 ) ये गजपत्र में प्रकाशित होने की नारीख को प्रवृत्त होगे । 
2. मिलाई मशीन निर्यात ( क्वालिटी नियन्त्रण और निरीक्षण ) नियम , 
1967 मे नियम 8 के स्थान पर निम्नलिखित नियम रखा जाएगा ,

___ } पद का दर्जा बढ़ाने , इसका वेतनमान बढ़ाने और उपर्युक्त कार्य 
का स्थानान्तरित करने के मम्मन्ध में जारी किये गये प्रादेण , उम तारीख 
में लागू होंगे जिम नारीन में श्री मिद्दिकी इम पद का कार्यभार संभालेंगे ।

अधिनियम, 1963 ( 1963 का 22) की धारा 17 द्वारा प्रदत्त 
शस्तियों का प्रयोग करते हए , केन्द्रीय गरफार पतवा छोटे औजार

संशोधन के लिए निम्नलिखित नियम बनानी है ;

निरीक्षण ) नियम , 1987 में नियम 8 के स्थान पर निम्नलिखित नियम

उसके द्वारा ऐसे इंफार की संसूचना प्राप्त होने के 10 दिन के

का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार डीजल इजिन निर्यात ( क्वालिटी 
नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण ) नियम , 1967 में और मागे मशोधन के लिए 
एमद्वारा निम्नलिखित नियम बनाती है ; 
1. ( 1 ) इन नियमों का नाम हीजल इजिन निर्यात ( क्वालिटी नियंत्रण 

और निरीक्षण मशोधन नियम , 1971 है । 
( 2 ) ये राजपन्न में प्रकाशित होने की तारीख को प्रवृत्त होंग । 
2 डीजल जिन निर्यात ( क्वालिटी नियन्त्रण और निरीक्षण ) नियम , 
1987, मे नियम 8 के स्थान पर निम्नलिखित नियम रखा जाएगा ,

" अपील--- ( 1 ) नियम 4 के उपनियम ( 4 ) के अधीन 
अभिकरण द्वारा प्रमाणपत्र देने के कार से व्यथित कोई व्यक्ति , 
उमके द्वारा ऐसे इंकार की संसूचना प्राप्त करने के 10 दिनों 
के भीतर, तीन से अन्यून किन्तु सात से अनधिक ऐसे विशेषज्ञों 
के पैनल की अपील कर मकेगा जो इन नियमो के अधीन मामलो 
से उद्भत अपीलों की सुनवाई और विनिश्चय के प्रयोजनार्थ 
केन्द्रीय सरकार द्वारा नियुक्त किए जाए ।

दो तिहाई गैर मरकारी सदस्यों का होगा । 

( 3 ) पैनल का कोरम तीन का होगा ।

( 5 ) पपील, उमके प्राप्त होने से पन्द्रह दिन के भीतर 
निपटाई जाएगी । "

7 के स्थान पर निम्नलिखित नियम रखा जाएगा , अर्थात् - -

उसके द्वारा ऐसे इंकार की मंसूचना प्राप्त होने के 10 दिन के 
भीतर , सीन से अन्यून किन्तु मात से अनधिक ऐसे विशेषज्ञों के

का० प्रा० 3400. - - मर्यश्री उमा इन्डस्ट्रीज , 5 - 3 - 673, शंकर 
बाग , हैदराबाद को राज्य व्यापार निगम भाग्न लि० से माल के 
प्राबंटन के लिये 1. डेडिमिल बेंजीन/ अल्काइल बेंजीन , 2. कास्टिक सोडा , 3. 
मोडा ऐश एवं 4. यूरिया ( रासायनों ) के लिये 55. 040 रु० का एक 
रिहाई प्रादेश म० पो / एम / पार/ 724768 दिनांक 30 - 3 - 74 म्वीकृत 
किया गया था । 

उन्होंने रिहाई प्रादेश की प्रभुलिपि प्रति के लिये इस प्राधार पर 
प्रावेदन किया है कि मूल रिहाई प्रादेश माल के संभरण के लिये 
प्रस्तुत किए बिना और उसका विस्कुल उपयोग किए बिना ही खो गया 
भथवा मस्थानस्थ हो गया है । 


( 2 ) विशेषज्ञो के पैनल की कुल मवस्यता का कम से कम 
यो तिहाई गैर -सरकारी मदस्यो का होगा । 

( 3 ) पैनल का कोरम तीन का होगा । 


( 4 ) ऐमा अपील पर पैनल का यिनिश्चय अन्तिम होगा । 


( 5 ) अपील , उमके प्राप्त होने से पन्द्रह दिन के भीतर 
निपटाई जाएगी ।

सी / एक्स एक्म / 50/ उम्म्यू / 37- 38 दिनांक 14- 2- 74 मूल्म 3,500 २० 

प्रादेग 
( तीन हजार पाच भौ रुपए मात्र ) स्वीकृत किया गया था । 

बंगलोर, 31 अगस्त , 1974 


उन्होंने प्राधिकार पत्र के माथ लाइसेंम की अनुलिपि सीमाशुल्क 
कार्यसम्बन्धी प्रति के लिए इस आधार पर आवेदन किया है कि प्राधिकार 
पत्र के साथ मूल लाइमेम किसी भी सीमाशुल्क प्राधिकारी के पास पंजी 
कुन कराए बिमा और बिल्कुल उपयोग किए बिना हो खो गया है । 


अपने तर्क के समर्थन में आवेदक ने एक शपप -पत्र दाखिल किया है । 
मैं सन्तुष्ट हूं कि प्राधिकार पत्र के माय लाइमस की मूल मीमाशुल्क 
कार्य सम्बन्धी प्रति खो गई है और निदेश देता हूं कि पावेदक को 
प्राधिकार पत्र के साथ लाइसेस की अनुलिपि सीमाशुल्क कार्यसम्बन्धी 
प्रति जारी की जानी चाहिए । प्राधिकार पत्र के साथ लाइम फी मूम 
सीमाशुल्क कार्यसम्बन्धी प्रति एवद्वारा रद्द की जाती है । 


का० प्रा० 3402. -- मर्षश्री डाली डेसेज शक्ति मेन्शन , 5, उत्तरादि मटूट गेस, 
चिकपेट, बंगलोर, 2 - ए को प्रौद्योगिक सिलाई मशीमों के फालतू पुजों के मायात 
में लिय 20, 000 रुपये का एक पायान लाइसेस संख्या पी / एस / 1829104/ 
मी / एक्स एक्म /50/37- 38,दिनांक 4 - 3 -74 स्वीकृत किया गया था । उन्होंने 
उफ लाहसेम की मीमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति की अनुलिपि प्रति के लिये इस 
प्राधार पर आवेदन किया है कि लाइसेंम की उपर्युक्त मूल सीमाशुल्क 
प्रयोगन प्रति किमी भी मीमाशुल्क प्राधिकारी के पास पंजीकृत कराए 
मिना और उसका बिलकुल उपयोग किए बिना ही खो गई है और अब 
उपर्युक्त लाइन्म की गोमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति की अनुलिपि प्रप्ति की 
अावश्यकता लाइमेन्म के पूरे मूल्य अर्थात् 20, 000 रुपये के लिये 
है । 


[ फा सं० सम्ल्यू - 7/ एस एस आई/ एन पी / ए एम 74/ 002904 / १५० ]

उपर्युक्त तर्क के समर्थन में पायेवफ ने एक शपथपत्र दाखिल किया है । 
मैं संतुष्ट है कि उक्त लाइसेम की मूल सीमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति खो गई 
है पौर निदेश देता हू कि प्रावेदक को उपयुक्त लाइसेम की मीमाशुरुक 
प्रयोजन प्रति की अनुलिपि प्रति जारी की जानी चाहिये । उक्त लाइसेंस 
की मूल सीमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति एम द्वारा रह की जाती है ।

का० प्रा० 3404 .-सर्वश्री नटराज अगरबत्ती , 10-4- 37/ 1 मजब, टैंक सरोजनी 
देवीमाई अस्पताल के पास , हैदराबाव- 28 को सामान्य मुद्रा क्षेत्र से अप्रैल -मार्च 
74 अवधि के लिए रेडबुक के अनुमार ( 1 ) मारणीयर एवं प्रतिबंधित 
मे भिन्न 1000 रुपए ( एक हजार रुपए मात्र ) तक स्वीकृत किस्म के

प्रापात लाइसेंस स० पी०/ एम/ 1742773/ सी / एक्स एक्म / 50/ पुरल्य / 37-38 
दिनांक 8- 1-74 स्वीकृत किया गया था । 


उन्होंने प्राधिकार पत्र की सीमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति के साथ लाइसेस 
की अनुलिपि मीमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति के लिए इम भाषार पर आवेदन 
किया है प्राधिकार पत्र के माथ मल लाइमेंम बिलकुल उपयोग किए 
बिना ही खो गया है । 


उन्होंने प्राधिकार पत्र की मीमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति के साथ लाइसेम 
की अनुलिपि सीमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति के लिए इस आधार पर प्रावेदन 
किया है कि प्राधिकार पत्र के माथ मूल लाइमेंम बिल्कुल उपयोग किए 
मिना ही खो गया है । 


अपने तर्क के समर्थन मे उन्होने एक शपथपत्र दाखिल किया 
है । मैं मंतुष्ट ह कि प्राधिकार पत्र के माथ लाइसेम की मूल भीमाशुल्क 
प्रयोजन प्रति खो गई है और निवेश देता है कि प्रावेदन को प्राधिकार 
पत्र के माथ लाइसेम की प्रमुलिपि मीमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति जारी की जानी 
चाहिए । प्राधिकार पत्र के माय लाटमम की मृल मीमाशुम्म प्रयोजन 
प्रनि एसदाग रह श्री प्राप्ती है । 


प्रपने मर्क के ममर्थन मे उन्होने एक शपय -पन्न दाखिल किया है । 
मैं संतुष्ट है कि प्राधिकार पत्र के माथ माइसेम की मूल सीमाशुल्क 
प्रयोजन प्रमि खो गई है और निवेश देता हूं कि प्रायवक को प्राधिकार 
पत्र के माथ लाइसेम की अनुलिपि मीमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति प्रारी की जानी 
चाहिए । प्राधिकार पत्र के माथ ग्नाइमेम की मूल मीमा/ गुल्क प्रयोजन 
प्रति एतद्वारा रद्द की जाती है ।

मा०मा० 340 5. -- सर्वश्री शक्ति इन्डस्ट्रीज, 10-5-21 अपोजिटस पुलिस 
माफिसर मेस मजय , टैक, हैदराबाव को सामान्य मुद्रा क्षेत्र से ( 1 ) अप्रैल-मार्च 74 
के प्रमुमार 1000 रुपए ( एक हजार रुपए मात्र ) तक मरणीबद्ध एवं प्रतिबंधित 
से भिन्न किस्म के प्राकृतिक सुगंधित सेल मधों एवं ( 2 ) 1000 रुपए 
तक थिमोल के प्रायात के लिए, 2000 रुपये ( बो हजार रुपए मात्र ) का एक 
पायात लाइसेस सं० 1742777/ सी / एक्स एक्स / 50/ डब्ल्यू / 37- 38 दिनांक 
8- 1-74 स्वीकृत किया गया था । 


उन्होंने प्राधिकार पत्र को सीमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति के साथ लाइसेस 
की अनुलिपि सीमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति के लिए इस आधार पर प्रावेदन 
किया है कि प्राधिकार पत्र के माथ मूल लाइसेंस बिल्कुल उपयोग किए 
बिना ही खो गया है । 


उन्होंने लाइसेंस की अनुलिपि मीमाणुस्क प्रयोजन प्रति के लिए श्म 
माधार पर प्रावेदन किया है कि मून लाइमेंस बिल्कुल उपयोग किए 
बिना ही खो गया है । 


अपने तर्क के समर्थन में उन्होंने एक शपथ -पत्र दाखिल किया है । 
मैं सन्तुष्ट है कि प्राधिकार पत्र के माथ लाइसेम की मूल सीमाशुल्क 
प्रयोजन प्रति खो गई है और निवेश देता हूं कि आवेदक को प्राधिकार 
पत्र के साथ लाइसेम की अनुलिपि मीमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति जारी की 
जानी चाहिए । प्राधिकार पत्र के साथ लाइमम को मूल मीमा शुल्क 
प्रयोजन प्रति एतद्वारा रद्द की गाती है । 


अपने तर्फ के समर्थन मे आवेदक ने एक शपथ-पत्र दाखिल किया है । 
में संतुष्ट हूं कि लाइसेस की मूल सीमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति खो गई है 
पोर निदेश देता हूँ कि आवेदक को लाइमेम की प्रमुलिपि मीमाशुल्क 
प्रति जारी की जानी चाहिए । लाइमेंम की मुल सीमाशुल्क प्रयोजन प्रति 
एतद्वारा रद्द की जाती है । 


[ संख्या/ पार- 97/ एम एस पाई/ एन पी / ए एम 73 / 003000/ हैव० ]

अतः, अब पेट्रोलियम पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के प्रकार का 
अर्मन ) अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधार। 
( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियो का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उसमें 
उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करने का अपमा प्राशय एतत् द्वारा घोषित 
किया है । 


[ No . 12016 /16 / 74- L & LI 


बगर्ने कि उक्त भूमि में हितबद्ध कोई व्यक्ति , उम भूमि के नीचें पाइप 
पाइन बिछाने के लिए प्राक्षेप सक्षम प्राधिकारी , जेल तथा प्राकृतिक गैम 
पायोग, निर्माण और देखभाल प्रभाग , मकरपुरा रोड़ बरौदा -9 को इस 
अधिसूचना की तारीख से 21 दिनो के भीतर कर सकेगा । 

और ऐमा प्राक्षेप करने वाला हर व्यक्ति विनिर्दिष्ट : यह भी कथन 
फरेगा कि क्या वह चाहता है कि उसकी सुनयाई व्यक्तिशः हो या फिमी 
विधि व्यवसायी की मार्फत । 


का० प्रा० 3417. — यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत 
होता है कि लोकहित में यह मावश्यक है कि गुजगत राज्य में जी एस एन 
के - 66 से जी जी एम से पार प्रो यू के साथ मिलाते हुए डी एस एन के 
59 से जी जी एस तफ पेट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए पाइप लाइन तेल 
मश्रा प्राकृतिक गैस प्रायोग द्वारा बिछाई जानी चाहिए । 


और यत: यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी लाइनो को बिछाने के प्रयोजन 
के लिए एतद्पाबर मनुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार अजित 
करना आवश्यक है ।

S. O . 3422. -- Whereas it appears to the Central Govern

का० प्रा० 3422.- - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता 
है कि लोकहित मे यह आवश्यक है कि गुजरात राज्य मे एन के - 51 
से एन के - 60 तक पैट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए पाइप लाइन तेल 
तथा प्राकृतिक गैस प्रायोग द्वारा बिछाई जानी चाहिए । 

और यन: यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐमी लाइनो को बिछाने के 
प्रयोजन के लिए एनदपायच अनुरूघी में यणित भूमि में उपयोग का 
अधिकार अर्जित करना प्रावश्यक है । 


And whereas it appears that for the purpose of laying 
such Pipelines, it is necessary to acquire the Right of User 
in the land described in the scheduled annexed hereto ; 


Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred 
by sub -section ( 1 ) of the section 3 of the Petroleum Pipe 
lines ( Acquisition of Right of User in land ) Act, 1962 (50 
of 1962 ) , the Central Government hereby declarea its in 
tention to acquire the right of user therein%3B 


अतः, अब पैट्रोलियम पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार 
फा अर्जन ) अधिनियम, 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदन शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हए केन्द्रीय 
मरकार ने उपमें उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करने का अपना प्राशय 
एतद द्वारा घोषित किया है । 


Provided that any person interested in the said land may, 
within 21 days from the date of this notification , object to 
the laying of the pipelines under tho land to the Com 
petent Authority , Oil & Natural Gas Commission, Construc 
tion & Maintenance Division, Makarpura Road , Baroda - 9 . 


बशर्ते कि उक्त भूमि मे हितबद्ध कोई व्यक्ति , उम भमि के 
नीचे पाइपलाइन बिछाने के लिए माक्षेप सक्षम प्राधिकारी, लेल तथा 
प्राकृतिक गैस प्रायोग, निर्माण और देखभाल प्रभाग , मकरपुग रोग 
परोदा- 9 को इस अधिसूचना की तारीख से 21 दिनों के भीतर कर 
सकेगा । 


And overy person making such an objection shall also 
state specifically whether he wishes to be heard in person or 
by a legal practitioner .

मा० प्रा० 3423. - - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता 
है कि लोकहित मे यह मावश्यक है कि गुजरात राज्य में एन के - 66 से 
जी जी / एस / सी टी एफ कादी तक पैट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए पाइप लाइन 
तेल तथा प्राकृतिक गैम भायोग द्वारा बिछाई जानी चाहिए । 

और यतः यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी लाइनों को मिछाने के 
प्रयोजन के लिए एतद्पाबद्ध अनुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में उपयोग का 
अधिकार अजित करना आवश्यक है 

प्रत:, अब पैट्रोलियम पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार 
का अर्जन ) अधिनियग, 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रक्स शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय 
सरकार ने उममें उपयोग का अधिकार अजिन करने का प्रपमा 
प्राशय एतवद्वारा घोषित किया है ।

पाइपलाइन बिछाने के लिए प्राक्षेप मक्षम , प्राधिकारी , तेल तथा प्राकृ 
तिक गैम प्रायोग , निर्माण और देखभाल प्रभाग, मकरपुरा रोड बरोदा- 9

And whereas it appears that for the purpose of laying such 
Pipelines, it is necessary to acquiro the Right of User in the 
land described in the schedule annexed hereto ;

section ( 1 ) of the section 3 of the Petroleum Pipelines (Acqui 
sition of Right of User in land) Act, 1962 ( 50 of 1962), the 
Central Government hereby declares its intention to acquic 
the right of user therein ;

गैस प्रायोग , निर्माण और देखभाल प्रभाग , मकरपुरा रोड , बरीवा-9 को 
हम अधिसूचना की तारीख से 21 विनो के भीतर कर सकेगा ।

का प्रा 343 5. - - अन्तर्राष्ट्रीय विमानपत्तन प्राधिकरण अधिनियम , 
1971 ( 1971 मा 43 ) की धारा 3 द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग 
करते हुए , केन्द्रीय सरकार एसद्वारा भारत अन्तर्गष्ट्रीय विमानपत्तन 
प्राधिकरण में इंजीनियरी के निदेशक , श्री पी० एम० मंधावालिया को 
13 दिसम्बर , 1971 में तीन वर्ष की अवधि के लिए अथवा प्राधिकरण 
का पुनर्गठन होने तक , जो भी पहले हा , भारत अन्तर्राष्ट्रीय विमानपत्तन

का० प्रा० 2436.- - मोरमुगाप्रो डॉक कर्मकार (नियोजन का विनियमन ) 
स्कीम , 1965 में और संशोधन करने के लिए स्कीम का निम्नलिखित 
प्रारूप, जिले केन्द्रीय मरकार, लॉक कर्मकार (नियोजन का विनियमन ) 
अधिनियम , 1948 ( 1948 का ५ ) की धारा 4 की उपधारा ( 1 ) 
वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए बनाने की प्रस्थापना करती 
है उक्त उपधारा की अपेक्षानुसार ऐसे मभी व्यक्तियों की जानकारी 
के लिए, जिनका उममे प्रभाधिन होना मम्भाव्य है , प्रकाशित किया जाता 
है ; और यह मूचना दी जाती है कि उक्त प्रारूप पर इस अधिसूचना , 
के गजपत्र में प्रकाशन की तारीख से वो माम के अवमान पर या उसके 
पश्चात विचार किया जाएगा । 

उक्त प्रारूप की बाबत जो प्राक्षेप या सुझाव किसी व्यक्ति में इस 
प्रकार विनिर्दिष्ट अवधि के पूर्व प्राप्त होंगे उन पर केन्द्रीय सरकार 
वारा विचार किया जाएगा । 

प्राम्प स्कीम 
1. इस स्कीम का नाम मोरमुगाप्रो डॉक कर्मकार (नियोजन का 
विनियमन ) सशोधन स्कीम , 1974 है ।

श्रम मन्त्रालय 

कंपनी ( प्राइवेट ) लिमिटेड के प्रबंधतन्त्र से सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उनके 

कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाय विद्यमान है ; 
आदेश 

और यत: केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए 
नई दिल्ली, 22 अक्तूबर , 1974 

निर्देशित करना वछिनीय समझती है । 
का० प्रा० 3446 - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे उपाय 

अतः, अब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) 
अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में श्री पान्हैयालाल , खान स्वामी , 

की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों 
ग्राम लम्बाकुवा , डाकघर दायी , जिला युन्नी ( राजस्थान ) से सम्बद्ध 
नियोजको और उनके कर्मचारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विधमान है । 

का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय मरकार उक्त विवाद को उमत अधिनियम 

की धारा 7क के अधीन गठित केन्द्रीय सरकार प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण 
और यत . केन्द्रीय सरकार उक विवाद को न्याय निर्णयन के लिए ( संख्या 1 ), बम्बई को न्यानिर्णयन के लिए निशित करती है । 
निवेशित करना छिनीय ममझती है ;

[ No. L 12012 / 50 / 74- LRIII ] 


प्रादेश 
का०मा० 3451 - यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार की गय है कि इसमे उपाबत 
प्रभुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में मेट्रल बैंक आफ इडिया से सम्बस 
नियोजकों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद विद्यमान है ; 

और यत : फेन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयम के लिए 
निर्देशित करना बांछनीय समझती है ; 

प्रतः, अब , प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) 
की धारा एक और धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खर ( प ) द्वारा 
प्रवस्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा एक 
प्रौद्योगिक मधिकरण गठित करती है, जिसके पीठासीन अधिकारी श्री एच ० 
भार० सोंधी होंगे जिनका मुख्यालय चन्डीगढ़ में होगा और उक्त विवाद 
को उक्त प्रौद्योगिक प्रधिकरण को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित करती है । 

अनुसूची 
स्या सेन्ट्रल बैंक प्राफ इंडिया , भांचलिक कार्यालय, चन्डीगढ़ के प्रबन्ध 
तंत्र का , रोहतक की सिविल लाइन शाखा के लिपिक एवं- ऐकक श्री पार० 
एन० माहूजा को प्रोन्नति के अवसर देने से इनकार करना न्यायोषित है? 
यदि मही, तो वह किस अनुतोष का हकदार है ? 

[ स० एल०-12012/ 74/ 74-एल०पार० III] 


प्रादेश 


नई दिल्ली , 25 नवम्बर, 1974 


ORDER 


S . 0 . 3451. - - Whereas the Central Government is of opin 
ion that an industrial dispute exists between the employers 
in relation to the Central Bank of India and their workmon 
in respect of the matters specified in the Schedule hercto 
annexed ; 


का०मा० 3450 -~-यत: केन्द्रीय सरकार की राय है कि इससे उपाय 
अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट विषयों के बारे में प्रोरियन्टल बैंक आफ कामर्स से 
सम्बद्ध नियोजकों और उसके कर्मकारों के बीच एक प्रौद्योगिक विवाद 
विद्यमान है ; 

और यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए 
निर्देशित करना वांछनीय समझती है ; 

प्रतः , अब, औद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 1947 ( 1947 का 14 ) 
की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 1 ) खण्ड ( घ ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का 
प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त विवाद को उक्त अधिनियम की 
धारा 7 के अधीन गठित केन्द्रीय मरकार औद्योगिक प्रधिकरण दिल्ली को 
न्यायनिर्णयन के लिए निर्देशित करती है । 

अनुचची 
क्या प्रोरियन्टल बैंक आफ कामर्स के प्रबन्धतंत्र की उस समय के 
विशेष सहायक श्री बी०पी० जैन को “ साहित्य रत्न " परीक्षा उत्तीर्ण करने 
के मध्ये , 1970 से दो वेतन वृद्धियां अनुदत्त न करने की कार्यवाही 
म्यायोचित है ? यदि नहीं , तो वह किस अनुतोष का हकदार है ? 

[ सं० ए०- 120 12/ 110/ 74- एल०प्रार० III ] 

ORDER 
Now Delhi, the 25th November, 1974 


And whereas the Central Government considers it desir 
able to refer the said dispute for adjudication ; 


Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred by 
section 7A and clause (d ) of sub -section ( 1 ) of section 10 of 
the Industrial Disputes Act , 1947 (14 of 1947), the Central 
Government hereby constitutes an Industrial Tribunal of 
which Shri H . R . Sondhi shall be the Presiding Officer with 
headquarters at Chandigarh and refers the said dispute for 
adjudication to the said Tribunal.

noved on this scoic alonc cannot be sustained

ORDER 
New Delhi, the 8th November , 1974 


S .O . 3457 . Whereas the Central Government is of opinion 
that an industrial dispute exists between the employers in 
relation to the Central Bank of India and their workman 
in respect of the matter specificd in the Schedulc hereto 
annexed : 


And, whereas the Central Govejnment considers it desis . 
able to refer the said dispute for adjudication ; 


Mr. Nair for Hardihar argued that there was no evidence 
that these adjustments were fictitious entries and further that 
theso letters had not been posted or had not been received . 
On the other hand, it was stated on behalf of the Bank that 
sone of the postal numbers in the adjusted entries were the 
same numbers which had been entered carlier in the register . 
He further argued that the same numbers could be repeated 
in the correspondence . In any case , I agreo with Mr. Nair s 
contention that it was not at all proved that Hardikar had 
embezzled any money . He may have acted foolishly in 
making some adjustments on the advice of Devgan . There 
appears to be force in the contention on bchalf of Hardikar 
that very likely it was agreed between the Union s reprc 
sentative and the Bank authorities that after Hardikar had 
gent a reply as suggested by the Union no further action 
would be taken . It may be that for some reasons the Bank 
authorities shifted their stand . In any case, I am not pre 
pared to uphold the findings of the Enquiry Officer which 
are clearly unjust. It would havo been in the Atness of 
things if the Bank had taken suitable departmental action 
against Hardikar for the irregularity committed by him in 
making adjustments . Warning or stoppage of increment for 
a period of six months might have been sufficient punishment. 
The Bank, however , preferred to act otherwise and decided 
to impose the extreme punishment. As I have mentioned , the 
procecdings before the Enquiry Officer were violative of the 
rules of natural justice and must be set aside , 


Now , therefore , in cxercise of the powers conferred by 
section 7A , and clause ( d ) of sub -section ( 1 ) of section 10 
of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 ( 14 of 1947 ) , the Con 
tral Government hereby constitutes an Industrial Tribunal 
of which Shri H . R . Sodhi shall be the Presiding Officer , 
with headquarters at Chandigarh and refers the said dispute 
for adjudication to tho said Tribunal. 


SCHEDULE 
Whether the action of the management of Central Bank 

of India in dçaying promotion to Shri K K . Trikha, 
Clerk , Rohtak Branch , under thc All India Service 
and under State Servico is justified ? If not, to 
what relief is he entitled ? 

[No. L - 12012 / 10 / 74 -LR.1111 


New Delhi, the 18th December, 1974 


My award , therefore , is that the action of the manage 
ment of State Bank of India , Jabalpur in discharging Shri V . 
D . Hardikar, Clerk - typist at their Medical College Branch , 
Jabalpur from service with effcct from the 6th July 1973 
was not justified . He is entitled to be reinstated in service 
from 6 - 7 - 1973 and should be paid all the amounts due to 
him as salary and allowances etc. The workman V . D . 
Hardikar will get Rs. 200 as costs from the Bank .

AWIRD 
The Central Government has made the following reference 
to this Tribunal for adjudication : 
" Whether the resignation tendred by Shri Hiralal. Sorter , 

Toonka Mine on the 6th June , 1969 was voluntary 
and its acceptancc by Messrs. Duduwala and Com 
pany, Mica Mine Owners , Bhilwara , was bonafide ! 
If not, to what relief is the workman entitled ?" 


3746 


THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : DECEMBER 28 , 1974 / PAUSA 7, 1896 


[PART U 


The statement of claim has been filed on behalf of Khan 
Mazdoor Congress , Bhilwara . It is alleged in the statement 
of claim that Shri Hiralal was appointed as Head Sorter 
In the Duduwala & Company, Mica Mines Owners, Bhil 
wara in 1964. The Manager Shri Kishan Lal issued a charge 
sheet for theft but no enquiry was held . He was threatened 
by the Manager from time to timo that the matter will be 
reported to tho Police if he did not leave the service. Afraid 
of this action on the part of the management Shri Hiralal 
tendered his resignation on 6th June, 1969. Subscquently 
he reported the matter to the Union that the management 
had obtained his resignation under duress and threat. The 
Union took up the matter with the Conciliation Officer and 
on the failure report of the Concillation Officer, the Central 
Government has made this reference . 


In reply on behalf of the management it is denied that 
the resignation was obtained under duress and threat. It is 
alleged that it was tendered voluntarily . 


" Whether the action of the management of Rajasthan 

Atomic Power Project, Post Office Anushakti, Via 
Kota in giving a notice of change dated 20th March , 
1973 under section 9A of the Industrial Disputes 
Act, 1947 , in so far as payment of overtimo allow 
ance to Staff Car Driver is concerned , is justified ? 

If not, to what relief are they entitled ?" 
The dispute is between the Staff Car Drivers of the Rajas 
than Atomic Power Project, horcinafter called the Project, 
and the management of the Project. It is one of the Pro 
jects under thọ Power Projects Engineering Division of the 
Department of Atomic Energy , Government of India . The 
Aforesaid Department is engaged in the development, control 
and use of atomic energy for the welfarc of the people of 
India and for peaceful purposes and for matters connectod 
therewith in terms of the Atomic Energy Act (33 of 1962 ) . 

The Rajasthan Anushakti Pariyojna Karamchari Sangh , 
which is a recognised Union of the employees of tho Pro 
jcct, hereinafter called the Union , had raised a dispute ro 
garding payment of overtime allowance to drivers at the 
ratcs paid to other employees of the project. Prior to 
1 - 8 - 1971 the drivers were being paid overtimo allowance in 
accordance with the Staff Car Rules. After negotiations , A 
conciliation settlement was signed before the Assistant 
Labour Commissioner (Central) , Kota on 17- 11- 1971 which 
provided for payment of overtime allowance to Motor Vehi 
cle Drivers and Bus Helpers of the Project in accordance 
with the provisions of the Motor Transport Workers Act, 
1961. 

The parties did not lead any oral evidence . Their learned 
representatives argued before me and also fled written ar 
guments . 


Evidence of both the parties was recorded . The work 
man produced two other witnesses Hiralal s / o Laluram and 
Nandram besidog himself , but they do not say that the re 
signation was written in their presence and under threat , 
There is the statement of the workman alone and he has 
stated that his signatures were obtained on the threat that 
the matter will be reported to the Polico . 


I have considered this cvidence and find that the fact that 
the signatures were obtained under duress or threat is not 
proved . The other witness produced by the workman do 
not help him . On the sole evidence of the workman I am 
not prepared to hold that the resignation was obtained by 
force . The contention of the workman is, therefore , re 
jccted and the reference is answered accordingly . The award 
bo sent to the Central Government for publication . 


U . N . MATHUR , Presiding Officer

entitled to overtime allowance as admissible to them under 
the Stall Car Rules and not under tho Motor Transport 
Workers Act, 1961. Thus a disputo was raised between the 
management of the Project and the Union and the parties 
agreed for a joint reference of the dispute for adjudication 
under section 10 ( 2 ) of the I. D . Act.

सेठ गिरधारी लाल दामोदर पास राठी ( एच० यू०एफ०) पोस्ट वापस नम्बर 6 
रायचूर नामक स्पापन से सम्बद नियोजक और कर्मचारियों की बहसंम्पा इस 
मात पर सहमत हो गई है कि कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि मौर कुटुम्ब पेंशन 
निधि अधिनियम , 1952 ( 1952 का 19 ) के उपबन्ध उक्त स्थापन 
को लागू किए जाने चाहिए ।

प्रदास शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त अधिनियम के 
उसमन्ध उक्त स्थापन को लागू करती है ।

का०मा० 3478 – यत . केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि 
मैसर्स मरोल कोमापरेटिव इंडस्ट्रियल एस्टेट , मरोल भयम , मथुच्चास 
पास्सनजी रोड , जे . बी . मगर डा०प० मुम्बई - 59 मामक म्पापन 
से सम्बद्ध नियोजक और कर्मचारियों की बहुसंख्या इस बात पर महमत हो 
गई है कि कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि पौर मुटुम्ब पेंशन निधि अधिनियम 
1952 ( 1952 का 19 ) के उपबंध उक्त स्थापन को लागू किए जाने 
पाहिए । 


प्रतः, अब , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 1 की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त पाक्सियो का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त अधिनियम के 
उपबन्ध उक्त स्थापन को लागू करती है ।
